An error is attached with the inaccessibility errors. Non-accessible results take priority over\n    ///                      non-viable results.\n    ///    a non-viable result - a result that means that the search continues into further scopes of lower priority for\n    ///                          a viable or non-accessible result. An error is attached with the error that indicates\n    ///                          why the result is non-viable.  A typical reason would be that it is the wrong kind of symbol.\n    /// \n    /// Note that the class is poolable so its instances can be obtained from a pool via GetInstance.\n    /// Also it is a good idea to call Free on instances after they no longer needed.\n    /// \n    /// The typical pattern is \"caller allocates / caller frees\" -\n    ///    \n    ///    var result = LookupResult.GetInstance();\n    ///  \n    ///    scope.Lookup(result, \"goo\");\n    ///    ... use result ...\n    ///         \n    ///    result.Clear();\n    ///    anotherScope.Lookup(result, \"moo\");\n    ///    ... use result ...\n    /// \n    ///    result.Free();   //result and its content is invalid after this\n    ///    \n    /// \n    /// \n    /// </summary>\n    /// <remarks>\n    /// Currently LookupResult is intended only for name lookup, not for overload resolution. It is\n    /// not clear if overload resolution will work with the structure as is, require enhancements,\n    /// or be best served by an alternate mechanism.\n    /// \n    /// We might want to extend this to a more general priority scheme.\n    /// \n    /// </remarks>\n    internal sealed class LookupResult\n    {\n        // the kind of result.\n        private LookupResultKind _kind;\n\n        // If there is more than one symbol, they are stored in this list.\n        private readonly ArrayBuilder<Symbol> _symbolList;\n\n        // the error of the result, if it is NonViable or Inaccessible\n        private DiagnosticInfo _error;\n\n        private readonly ObjectPool<LookupResult> _pool;\n\n        private LookupResult(ObjectPool<LookupResult> pool)\n        {\n            _pool = pool;\n            _kind = LookupResultKind.Empty;\n            _symbolList = new ArrayBuilder<Symbol>();\n            _error = null;\n        }\n\n        internal bool IsClear\n        {\n            get\n            {\n                return _kind == LookupResultKind.Empty && _error == null && _symbolList.Count == 0;\n            }\n        }\n\n        internal void Clear()\n        {\n            _kind = LookupResultKind.Empty;\n            _symbolList.Clear();\n            _error = null;\n        }\n\n        internal LookupResultKind Kind\n        {\n            get\n            {\n                return _kind;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Return the single symbol if there is exactly one, otherwise null.\n        /// </summary>\n        internal Symbol SingleSymbolOrDefault\n        {\n            get\n            {\n                return (_symbolList.Count == 1) ? _symbolList[0] : null;\n            }\n        }\n\n        internal ArrayBuilder<Symbol> Symbols\n        {\n            get\n            {\n                return _symbolList;\n            }\n        }\n\n        internal DiagnosticInfo Error\n        {\n            get\n            {\n                return _error;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Is the result viable with one or more symbols?\n        /// </summary>\n        internal bool IsMultiViable\n        {\n            get\n            {\n                return Kind == LookupResultKind.Viable;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// NOTE: Even there is a single viable symbol, it may be an error type symbol.\n        /// </summary>\n        internal bool IsSingleViable\n        {\n            get\n            {\n                return Kind == LookupResultKind.Viable && _symbolList.Count == 1;\n            }\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult Good(Symbol symbol)\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.Viable, symbol, null);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult WrongArity(Symbol symbol, DiagnosticInfo error)\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.WrongArity, symbol, error);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult Empty()\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.Empty, null, null);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult NotReferencable(Symbol symbol, DiagnosticInfo error)\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.NotReferencable, symbol, error);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult StaticInstanceMismatch(Symbol symbol, DiagnosticInfo error)\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.StaticInstanceMismatch, symbol, error);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult Inaccessible(Symbol symbol, DiagnosticInfo error)\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.Inaccessible, symbol, error);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult NotInvocable(Symbol unwrappedSymbol, Symbol symbol, bool diagnose)\n        {\n            var diagInfo = diagnose ? new CSDiagnosticInfo(ErrorCode.ERR_NonInvocableMemberCalled, unwrappedSymbol) : null;\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.NotInvocable, symbol, diagInfo);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult NotLabel(Symbol symbol, DiagnosticInfo error)\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.NotLabel, symbol, error);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult NotTypeOrNamespace(Symbol symbol, DiagnosticInfo error)\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.NotATypeOrNamespace, symbol, error);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult NotTypeOrNamespace(Symbol unwrappedSymbol, Symbol symbol, bool diagnose)\n        {\n            // TODO: determine correct diagnosis \n            var diagInfo = diagnose ? new CSDiagnosticInfo(ErrorCode.ERR_BadSKknown, unwrappedSymbol.Name, unwrappedSymbol.GetKindText(), MessageID.IDS_SK_TYPE.Localize()) : null;\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.NotATypeOrNamespace, symbol, diagInfo);\n        }\n\n        internal static SingleLookupResult NotAnAttributeType(Symbol symbol, DiagnosticInfo error)\n        {\n            return new SingleLookupResult(LookupResultKind.NotAnAttributeType, symbol, error);\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Set current result according to another.\n        /// </summary>\n        internal void SetFrom(SingleLookupResult other)\n        {\n            _kind = other.Kind;\n            _symbolList.Clear();\n            _symbolList.Add(other.Symbol);\n            _error = other.Error;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Set current result according to another.\n        /// </summary>\n        internal void SetFrom(LookupResult other)\n        {\n            _kind = other._kind;\n            _symbolList.Clear();\n            _symbolList.AddRange(other._symbolList);\n            _error = other._error;\n        }\n\n        internal void SetFrom(DiagnosticInfo error)\n        {\n            this.Clear();\n            _error = error;\n        }\n\n        // Merge another result with this one, with the current result being prioritized\n        // over the other if they are of equal \"goodness\". Mutates the current result.\n        internal void MergePrioritized(LookupResult other)\n        {\n            if (other.Kind > Kind)\n            {\n                SetFrom(other);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Merge another result with this one, with the symbols combined if both\n        /// this and other are viable. Otherwise the highest priority result wins (this if equal \n        /// priority and non-viable.)\n        /// </summary>\n        internal void MergeEqual(LookupResult other)\n        {\n            if (Kind > other.Kind)\n            {\n                return;\n            }\n            else if (other.Kind > Kind)\n            {\n                this.SetFrom(other);\n            }\n            else if (Kind != LookupResultKind.Viable)\n            {\n                // this makes the operator not symmetrical, but so far we do not care.\n                // it is really a matter of which error gets reported.\n                return;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                // Merging two viable results together. We will always end up with at least two symbols.\n                _symbolList.AddRange(other._symbolList);\n            }\n        }\n\n        internal void MergeEqual(SingleLookupResult result)\n        {\n            if (Kind > result.Kind)\n            {\n                // existing result is better\n            }\n            else if (result.Kind > Kind)\n            {\n                this.SetFrom(result);\n            }\n            else if ((object)result.Symbol != null)\n            {\n                // Same goodness. Include all symbols\n                _symbolList.Add(result.Symbol);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // global pool\n        //TODO: consider if global pool is ok.\n        private static readonly ObjectPool<LookupResult> s_poolInstance = CreatePool();\n\n        // if someone needs to create a pool\n        internal static ObjectPool<LookupResult> CreatePool()\n        {\n            ObjectPool<LookupResult> pool = null;\n            pool = new ObjectPool<LookupResult>(() => new LookupResult(pool), 128); // we rarely need more than 10\n            return pool;\n        }\n\n        internal static LookupResult GetInstance()\n        {\n            var instance = s_poolInstance.Allocate();\n            Debug.Assert(instance.IsClear);\n            return instance;\n        }\n\n        internal void Free()\n        {\n            this.Clear();\n            if (_pool != null)\n            {\n                _pool.Free(this);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n","numLines":325,"startLine":1,"totalLines":325}}}

An error is attached with the inaccessibility errors. Non-accessible results take priority over\n    30→    ///                      non-viable results.\n    31→    ///    a non-viable result - a result that means that the search continues into further scopes of lower priority for\n    32→    ///                          a viable or non-accessible result. An error is attached with the error that indicates\n    33→    ///                          why the result is non-viable.  A typical reason would be that it is the wrong kind of symbol.\n    34→    /// \n    35→    /// Note that the class is poolable so its instances can be obtained from a pool via GetInstance.\n    36→    /// Also it is a good idea to call Free on instances after they no longer needed.\n    37→    /// \n    38→    /// The typical pattern is \"caller allocates / caller frees\" -\n    39→    ///    \n    40→    ///    var result = LookupResult.GetInstance();\n    41→    ///  \n    42→    ///    scope.Lookup(result, \"goo\");\n    43→    ///    ... use result ...\n    44→    ///         \n    45→    ///    result.Clear();\n    46→    ///    anotherScope.Lookup(result, \"moo\");\n    47→    ///    ... use result ...\n    48→    /// \n    49→    ///    result.Free();   //result and its content is invalid after this\n    50→    ///    \n    51→    /// \n    52→    /// \n    53→    /// </summary>\n    54→    /// <remarks>\n    55→    /// Currently LookupResult is intended only for name lookup, not for overload resolution. It is\n    56→    /// not clear if overload resolution will work with the structure as is, require enhancements,\n    57→    /// or be best served by an alternate mechanism.\n    58→    /// \n    59→    /// We might want to extend this to a more general priority scheme.\n    60→    /// \n    61→    /// </remarks>\n    62→    internal sealed class LookupResult\n    63→    {\n    64→        // the kind of result.\n    65→        private LookupResultKind _kind;\n    66→\n    67→        // If there is more than one symbol, they are stored in this list.\n    68→        private readonly _kind = LookupResultKind.Empty;\n    79→            _symbolList = new ArrayBuilder<Symbol>();\n    80→            _error = null;\n    81→        }\n    82→\n    83→        internal bool IsClear\n    84→        {\n    85→            get\n    86→            {\n    87→                return _kind == LookupResultKind.Empty && _error == null && _symbolList.Count == 0;\n    88→            }\n    89→        }\n    90→\n    91→        internal void Clear()\n    92→        {\n    93→            _kind = LookupResultKind.Empty;\n    94→            _symbolList.Clear();\n    95→            _error = null;\n KEY LOOKUP METHODS AND SIGNATURES\n\n**Primary Lookup Entry Points:**\n\n- **`LookupSymbolsInternal()`** (lines 81-113)\n  - Walks through binder scope chain: `for (var scope = this; scope != null && !result.IsMultiViable; scope = scope.Next)`\n  - Stops when finding viable results or reaching end of chain\n  - Signature: `Binder LookupSymbolsInternal(LookupResult result, string name, int arity, ConsList<TypeSymbol> basesBeingResolved, LookupOptions options, bool diagnose, ref CompoundUseSiteInfo<AssemblySymbol> useSiteInfo)`\n\n- **`LookupSymbolsInSingleBinder()`** (virtual at line 115-118)\n  - Core override point for subclasses to implement scope-specific lookup\n  - Base Binder implementation is empty; overridden in LocalScopeBinder, InContainerBinder, etc.\n  - Signature: `virtual void LookupSymbolsInSingleBinder(LookupResult result, string name, int arity, ConsList<TypeSymbol> basesBeingResolved, LookupOptions options, Binder originalBinder, bool diagnose, ref CompoundUseSiteInfo<AssemblySymbol> useSiteInfo)`\n\n- **`LookupSymbolsOrMembersInternal()`** (lines 125-143)\n  - Dispatcher: handles both unqualified lookup and member lookup\n  - Routes to either `LookupSymbolsInternal()` or `LookupMembersInternal()` based on `qualifierOpt`\n\n- **`LookupMembersInternal()`** (lines 164-177)\n  - Looks up members in a specific namespace or type\n  - Routes to type-specific handlers: `LookupMembersInType()`, `LookupMembersInNamespace()`\n\n**Type-Specific Member Lookups:**\n\n- **`LookupMembersInType()`** (lines 180-218)\n  - Dispatcher based on `TypeKind` (Interface, Class, Struct, Enum, Delegate, Array, Dynamic, TypeParameter, etc.)\n\n- **`LookupMembersInClass()`** (lines 793-861)\n  - Walks inheritance chain: `while ((object)currentType != null)`\n  - Applies hiding rules: methods hide non-methods, non-methods hide everything\n  - Short-circuits on viable non-method results\n  - Handles base type traversal with `currentType.GetNextBaseTypeNoUseSiteDiagnostics()`\n\n- **`LookupMembersInInterface()`** (lines 1150-1165)\n  - First looks in the interface itself via `LookupMembersInInterfaceOnly()`\n  - Then includes System.Object members via `LookupMembersInClass()` on `System.Object`\n\n- **`LookupMembersInInterfaceOnly()`** (lines 1012-1034)\n  - Looks in interface and base interfaces via `LookupMembersInInterfacesWithoutInheritance()`\n\n- **`LookupMembersInTypeParameter()`** (lines 1168-1185)\n  - Looks in effective base class and effective interfaces\n  - Uses `AllEffectiveInterfacesWithDefinitionUseSiteDiagnostics()` to avoid explicit implementations\n\n- **`LookupMembersWithoutInheritance()`** (lines 763-775, static)\n  - Gets candidate members via `GetCandidateMembers()`\n  - Checks viability of each via `CheckViability()`\n  - Merges results: `result.MergeEqual(resultOfThisMember)`\n\n**Extension Methods:**\n\n- **`LookupExtensionMethods()`** (lines 47-53)\n  - Iterates through `ExtensionMethodScopes` enumerator\n  - For each scope, calls `LookupExtensionMethodsInSingleBinder()`\n\n- **`LookupExtensionMethodsInSingleBinder()`** (lines 460-473)\n  - Gets extension methods via `binder.GetCandidateExtensionMethods()`\n  - Checks viability and merges results\n\n---\n\n### 2. SCOPE WALKING PATTERNS\n\n**Scope Chain Walking:**\n\nThe Binder class uses a linked-list pattern where each Binder has a `Next` property:\n\n```\nBinder.Next -> Binder.Next -> Binder.Next -> ... -> BuckStopsHereBinder (null)\n```\n\n**Pattern in `LookupSymbolsInternal()` (lines 88-111):**\n\n```csharp\nBinder binder = null;\nfor (var scope = this; scope != null && !result.IsMultiViable; scope = scope.Next)\n{\n    if (binder != null)\n    {\n        var tmp = LookupResult.GetInstance();\n        scope.LookupSymbolsInSingleBinder(tmp, ...);\n        result.MergeEqual(tmp);      // Merge with higher-priority results\n        tmp.Free();\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        scope.LookupSymbolsInSingleBinder(result, ...);\n        if (!result.IsClear)\n        {\n            binder = scope;           // Track first non-empty scope\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if ((options & LookupOptions.LabelsOnly) != 0 && scope.IsLastBinderWithinMember())\n    {\n        break;  // Labels don't cross member boundaries\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Key Points:**\n- Early exit when `IsMultiViable` is found\n- Different handling for first scope vs. subsequent scopes (pooled temp results)\n- Special handling for label lookups (stops at member boundary)\n\n**Hierarchy Walking in Classes (lines 812-857):**\n\n```csharp\nTypeSymbol currentType = type;\nwhile ((object)currentType != null)\n{\n    LookupMembersWithoutInheritance(tmp, currentType, ...);\n    MergeHidingLookupResults(result, tmp, ...);  // Apply hiding rules\n    \n    // Check if we can stop early\n    if (result.IsMultiViable && (tmpHidesMethodOrIndexers || !IsMethodOrIndexer(result.Symbols[0])))\n    {\n        break;\n    }\n    \n    // Move to next base type\n    currentType = currentType.GetNextBaseTypeNoUseSiteDiagnostics(basesBeingResolved, ...);\n}\n```\n\n**Interface Walking (lines 1136-1144):**\n\n```csharp\nforeach (NamedTypeSymbol baseInterface in interfaces)\n{\n    if (seenInterfaces is null || seenInterfaces.Add(baseInterface))\n    {\n        LookupMembersWithoutInheritance(tmp, baseInterface, ...);\n        MergeHidingLookupResults(current, tmp, ...);\n        tmp.Clear();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 3. LOOKUP RESULT STRUCTURE AND MERGING\n\n**`LookupResult` Class** (LookupResult.cs, lines 14-323):\n\nKey fields and properties:\n- `LookupResultKind _kind` - Indicates quality of result (see below)\n- `ArrayBuilder<Symbol> _symbolList` - May contain multiple symbols\n- `DiagnosticInfo _error` - Error info for non-viable results\n- `IsClear` - True if empty with no error\n- `SingleSymbolOrDefault` - Returns single symbol or null\n- `IsMultiViable` - True if kind == Viable with one or more symbols\n\n**Result Priority Hierarchy** (LookupResultKind.cs, lines 38-67):\n\nOrdered from lowest to highest priority:\n```\nEmpty < NotATypeOrNamespace < NotAnAttributeType < WrongArity < \nNotCreatable < Inaccessible < NotReferencable < NotAValue < \nNotAVariable < NotInvocable < NotLabel < StaticInstanceMismatch < \nOverloadResolutionFailure < Ambiguous < MemberGroup < Viable\n```\n\n**Merging Strategies:**\n\n1. **`MergeEqual(LookupResult other)`** (lines 256-277)\n   - Used when scopes have equal priority\n   - If both viable: combines symbols\n   - Otherwise: highest priority wins, \"this\" wins on ties\n\n2. **`MergePrioritized(LookupResult other)`** (lines 243-249)\n   - Used across different priority levels\n   - If `other.Kind > this.Kind`: takes entire result from `other`\n\n3. **`MergeHidingLookupResults()`** (lines 1224-1278)\n   - Applied between inheritance levels\n   - Non-methods hide everything\n   - Methods hide non-methods\n   - Methods of same name don't hide each other (handled later in overload resolution)\n   - Checks inheritance relationships to determine if hiding applies\n\n---\n\n### 4. originalBinder.IsEarlyAttributeBinder)\n        return ((NamedTypeSymbol)nsOrType).GetEarlyAttributeDecodingMembers(name);\n\n    // Labels don't have members\n    if ((options & LookupOptions.LabelsOnly) != 0)\n        return ImmutableArray<Symbol>.Empty;\n\n    // Primary constructor special case\n    if (nsOrType is SourceMemberContainerTypeSymbol { HasPrimaryConstructor: true } sourceMemberContainerTypeSymbol)\n        return sourceMemberContainerTypeSymbol.GetCandidateMembersForLookup(name);\n\n    // Standard member lookup\n    return nsOrType.GetMembers(name);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### FILE REFERENCES\n\n- **Main Lookup Implementation:** `/workspace/src/Compilers/CSharp/Portable/Binder/Binder_Lookup.cs` (2050 lines)\n- **Result This refactoring extracts these patterns into a reusable strategy-based approach, improving maintainability and reducing duplication.\n\n## Key Findings from Analysis\n\n### Common Patterns Identified\n1. **Scope Walking**: All lookups iterate through scope chain via `Binder.Next` until finding viable results\n2. **Result Merging**: Use `LookupResult.MergeEqual()` to combine results from different scopes\n3. **Viability Checking**: Apply `CheckViability()` to filter results by `LookupOptions`\n4. **Two-phase Lookup**: Optimize by first attempting without diagnostics, retrying with diagnostics only if needed\n5. **Early Exit**: Stop iteration when `IsMultiViable` result found\n\n### Different Resolution Contexts\n- **Unqualified Name Lookup** (`LookupSymbolsInternal`): Walk scope chain, search locals/labels/members\n- **Member Access** (`LookupMembersInternal`): Walk type hierarchy, apply hiding rules\n- **Extension Methods** (`LookupExtensionMethods`): Iterate through namespace scopes\n- **Namespace Resolution**: Walk namespace hierarchy respecting using directives\n- **Type Resolution**: Resolve types in hierarchies and constraint contexts\n\n## Implementation Strategy\n\n### 1.
